however, if the national court has serious doubts as to the validity of the Community act on which national act is based, it may exceptionally suspend temporarily the implementation of that act or in that regard take any other precautionary measure.
is then obliged to ask the question concerning the validity of the Court and to state reasons why it considers that the Community act is not valid.

63 The Court of First Instance erred in law in finding that, since the marks in question are not similar, it is not necessary to examine the good name of the earlier marks.
: The fact that the earlier mark of Calvin Klein is generally known, according to the appellant, should be taken into account for the purposes of determining whether more extensive protection should be granted to it.
: Good name of the earlier mark must be assessed in assessing similarity between marks and not after the similarity has been shown.
, in that regard, the appellant submits that if the trade mark is protected only if a third party of the ‘cK’ would have used the same way of graphic formation, it would mean that the trade mark of the CK with a good name would be granted a level of protection which is practically lower than the level of protection which would have been used if it were not generally known.
: The fact that the trade mark cK, as well as other distinct graphic representation of those letters, is famous to them, confers them to protect the use of the letter ‘CK’ in the fashion sector, since these letters identify Calvin Klein, in that sector, so that their use by a third party is confusing confusion through association.
